     available_from: form.available_from ? new Date(form.available_from).toISOString() : null,\r\n        available_until: form.available_until ? new Date(form.available_until).toISOString() :  };\r\n\r\n      let { error } = await executeUpsert(payload);\r\n      // Graceful fallback if background_avatar_url column hasn't been added via SQL yet\r\n      if (error && String(error.message || '').includes('background_avatar_url')) {\r\n        const { background_avatar_url, ...fallbackPayload } = payload;\r\n        const retryRes = await executeUpsert(fallbackPayload);\r\n        error = retryRes.error;\r\n      }\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      if (form.product_type === 'bundle') {\r\n        const sk1 = skins.find(s => String(s.id) === String(cardSkinIds[0]));\r\n        const sk2 = skins.find(s => String(s.id) === String(cardSkinIds[1]));\r\n\r\n        await supabase.from('themed_annual_bundles').upsert(\r\n          [\r\n            {\r\n              title:    original_price_dollars: normalPrice || listedPrice,\r\n              card_skin_id: cardSkinIds[0] || null,\r\n              card_skin_name: sk1?.name || null,\r\n              card_skin_url:          avatar_urls: avatarUrls,\r\n              creator_autograph: autographCreators[0] || null,\r\n              creator_autograph_2: autographCreators[1] || null,\r\n              bonus_hypes: payload.available_until,\r\n              is_active: publishActiveStatus\r\n            }\r\n          ],\r\n          { onConflict: 'title' }\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (setIsDirty) setIsDirty(false);\r\n      setIsComposerOpen(false);\r\n      await fetchCatalogAndVault();\r\n      showToast(\r\n        publishActiveStatus\r\n          ? `Published \"${payload.title}\" to the {\r\n      setSaving(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleToggleActive = async (prod: any) => {\r\n    const nextStatus = !prod.is_active;\r\n    await supabase\r\n      .from('in_app_store_products')\r\n      .update({ is_active: nextStatus })\r\n      .eq('id', prod.id);\r\n    if (prod.product_type === 'bundle') {\r\n      await supabase\r\n        .from('themed_annual_bundles')\r\n        .update({ is_active: nextStatus })\r\n        .eq('title', prod.title);\r\n    }\r\n    fetchCatalogAndVault();\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleDeleteProduct = async (prod: any) => {\r\n    if (\r\n      !window.confirm(\r\n        `Remove \"${prod.title}\" from the In-App Store? navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);\r\n    setCopiedId(prod.id);\r\n    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2000);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // ============================================================================\r\n  // INTERACTIVE SIGNATURE STUDIO HANDLERS\r\n  // ============================================================================\r\n  const openSignatureStudioById = (skinId: string) => {\r\n    const found = skins.find(s => String(s.id) === String(skinId));\r\n    if (!found) return alert('Select a Card Skin from the dropdown first.');\r\n    const existing = Array.isArray(found.signature_stamps) ? found.signature_stamps : [];\r\n    setEditingStampSkin(found);\r\n    setActiveStamps([...existing]);\r\n    setSelectedStampId(existing[0]?.id || null);\r\n    setSelectedSigToAdd(Object.keys(signatureMap)[0] || '');\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleAddSignatureStamp = () => {\r\n    if (!selectedSigToAdd || !signatureMap[selectedSigToAdd]) {\r\n      return alert('Select a creator signature from the dropdown.');\r\n    }\r\n    const setSelectedStampId(newStamp.id);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handlePointerMoveOnCard = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\r\n    if (!draggingStampId || !cardPreviewRef.current) return;\r\n    const rect = cardPreviewRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\r\n    const relX = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;\r\n    const relY = ((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;\r\n    const clampedX = Math.max(5, Math.min(95, Math.round(relX * 10) / 10));\r\n    const clampedY = Math.max(5, Math.min(95, Math.round(relY * 10) / 10));\r\n\r\n    setActiveStamps(prev =>\r\n      prev.map(st => (st.id === draggingStampId ? { ...st, x: clampedX, y: clampedY } : st))\r\n    );\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleSaveStampsToSkin = async () => {\r\n    if (!editingStampSkin) return;\r\n ].filter(Boolean);\r\n\r\n  const activeFrostedBgUrl =\r\n    form.background_avatar_url && activeFormAvatars.includes(form.background_avatar_url)\r\n      ? form.background_avatar_url\r\n      :     "supabase/functions/shopify-webhook/index.ts": "// @ts-nocheck\r\nimport { createClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\";\r\n\r\nconst supabase = createClient(\r\n  Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\") ?? \"\",\r\n  Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\") ?? \"\"\r\n);\r\n\r\n// Cryptographic HMAC-SHA256 verification so fake orders can never be spoofed\r\nasync function verifyShopifyHmac(rawBody: string, hmacHeader: string, secret: string): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (!hmacHeader || !secret) return false;\r\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\r\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\r\n    \"raw\",\r\n    encoder.encode(secret.trim()),\r\n    { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\r\n    false,\r\n    [\"sign\"]\r\n  );\r\n  const sigBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(rawBody));\r\n  const base64Sig = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(sigBuffer)));\r\n  return base64Sig === hmacHeader.trim();\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction generateUniquePromoCode(): string {\r\n  const chars = \"ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789\";\r\n  const segment = () =>\r\n    Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)]).join(\"\");\r\n  return segment() + \"-\" + segment() + \"-\" + segment();\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  try {\r\n    const rawBody = await req.text();\r\n    const shopifyHmac = req.headers.get(\"x-shopify-hmac-sha256\") ?? product\r\n  const isProductOwnedByUser = useCallback(\r\n    (prod: any): boolean => {\r\n      if (!prod || !currentUser) return false;\r\n\r\n      if (purchasedProductIds.includes(String(prod.id))) return true;\r\n\r\n      const skinIds: string[] = Array.isArray(prod.card_skin_ids)\r\n        ? prod.card_skin_ids.filter(Boolean).map(String)\r\n        : [];\r\n      const prodSkins = skinIds\r\n        .map(id => (cardSkins || []).find((s: any) => String(s.id) === String(id)))\r\n        .filter(Boolean);\r\n      const avatars: string[] =        avatars.length > 0 && avatars.every(url => unlockedAvatars.includes(String(url)));\r\n\r\n      if (prod.product_type === 'bundle') {\r\n        if (skinIds.length > 0 && avatars.length > 0)      .eq('user_id', userId)\r\n        .eq('product_id', String(prod.id))\r\n        .limit(1)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n      if (existingPurchase) {\r\n        setPurchasedProductIds(prev =>       : [];\r\n      const nextSkins = Array.from(\r\n        new Set([...existingSkins, ...skinIds.map(String), ...skinUrls].filter(Boolean))\r\n      );\r\n\r\n      const existingAvatars: string[]     JSON.stringify(Array.from(new Set([...savedHunts, ...prodAutographs])))\r\n          );\r\n        }\r\n        localStorage.setItem(`am_unlocked_skins_${userId}`, JSON.stringify(nextSkins));\r\n      } catch {\r\n        // ignore\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const bonusVaulted = Number(prod.bonus_vaulted_hypes || 0);\r\n      const currentVaulted = Number(prof.vaulted_hypes || 0);\r\n      const nextVaulted = currentVaulted + bonusVaulted;\r\n\r\n      const patch: Record<string, any> = {\r\n        unlocked_skins: nextSkins,\r\n        unlocked_avatars: nextAvatars,\r\n        unlocked_autographs: nextAutographs,\r\n        vaulted_hypes: nextVaulted\r\n      };\r\n\r\n      if (prod.include_1yr_sub) {\r\n        patch.is_premium = true;\r\n        patch.subscription_tier = patch.hypes_remaining = toppedActive;\r\n        patch.super_hypes_left = toppedActive;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (skinUrls[0]) {\r\n        patch.card_skin = skinUrls[0];\r\n supabase\r\n        .from('profiles')\r\n        .update(patch)\r\n        .eq('id', userId);\r\n\r\n      if (updateErr) {\r\n        const fallbackPatch: Record<string, any> = {\r\n          vaulted_hypes: nextVaulted\r\n        };\r\n        if (prod.include_1yr_sub) {\r\n          fallbackPatch.is_premium = true;\r\n          fallbackPatch.hypes_remaining = patch.hypes_remaining;\r\n          fallbackPatch.super_hypes_left = patch.super_hypes_left;\r\n        }\r\n        if (skinUrls[0]) fallbackPatch.card_skin = skinUrls[0];\r\n        if (prodAvatars[0] && !prof.avatar_url) ALL paid store products (Bundles, Skins & Avatar Packs)\r\n      const gross = Math.max(0, Number(prod.listed_price_dollars || 0));\r\n      if (recordLedger && gross > 0) {\r\n        const stripeFee = Number((gross * 0.029 + 0.30).toFixed(2));\r\n        const commCreators: string[] = Array.isArray(prod.commission_creators)\r\n          ? prod.commission_creators.filter(Boolean)\r\n          : [];\r\n        const isBundle = prod.product_type === 'bundle';\r\n        const creatorBounty =\r\n          isBundle && commCreators.length > 0\r\n            ? Number(prod.dev_allocation_dollars)\r\n            : isBundle && prod.include_1yr_sub\r\n            ? 1.50\r\n            : Number((gross * 0.10).toFixed(2));\r\n\r\n        const netSatAm = Math.max(\r\n          0,\r\n          Number((gross - stripeFee - creatorBounty - devAllocation).toFixed(2))\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        await supabase.from('store_payout_ledger').insert([\r\n          {\r\n            user_id: userId,\r\n            user_email: prof.email || null,\r\n            product_id: String(prod.id),\r\n            product_title: prod.title,\r\n            product_type: prod.product_type || 'bundle',\r\n            gross_amount_dollars: gross,\r\n            stripe_fee_dollars: stripeFee,\r\n            creator_bounty_dollars: creatorBounty,\r\n            creator_names: commCreators,\r\n            dev_allocation_dollars: devAllocation,\r\n            net_saturday_am_dollars: netSatAm,\r\n            status: 'completed'\r\n          }\r\n        ]);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      setPurchasedProductIds(prev => Array.from(new Set([...prev, String(prod.id)])));\r\n      setUnlockedSkins(nextSkins);\r\n      setUnlockedAvatars(nextAvatars);\r\n      setStoreToast(\r\n        `🎉 Unlocked \"${prod.title}\"! ${\r\n          bonusVaulted > 0 ? `+${bonusVaulted} Gold Hypes & ` : ''\r\n        }Your new items are ready in your Profile Loadout!`\r\n useEffect(() => {\r\n    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\r\n    const successProdId =\r\n      params.get('store_product_success') ||\r\n      sessionStorage.getItem('am_pending_store_product_success');\r\n\r\n    if (!successProdId || storeProducts.length === 0) return;\r\n    if (processedSuccessRef.current === successProdId) String(p.id).startsWith(successProdId.slice(0, 8))\r\n          );\r\n\r\n    if (!matchedProd) return;\r\n    processedSuccessRef.current = successProdId;\r\n const singleItemsList = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (activeCategory === 'bundles') return [];\r\n    return visibleProducts.filter(p => {\r\n      if (p.product_type === 'bundle') return false;\r\n setLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchPayoutData();\r\n  }, [fetchPayoutData]);\r\n\r\n  // Calculate Store & Bundle Economics Totals from store_payout_ledger\r\n  const storeEconomicsSummary = useMemo(() => {\r\n    let grossTotal = 0;\r\n    let stripeFeesTotal = 0;\r\n    let creatorBountiesTotal = 0;\r\n    let devAllocationTotal = 0;\r\n    let netSatAmTotal = 0;\r\n\r\n    const creatorBundleBountyMap = new Map<string, { name: string; totalBounty: number; salesCount: number }>();\r\n\r\n    storeLedger.forEach((row: any) => {\r\n      const gross = Number(row.gross_amount_dollars || 0);\r\n      const fee = Number(row.stripe_fee_dollars || 0);\r\n      const bounty = Number(row.creator_bounty_dollars || 0);\r\n      const dev = Number(row.dev_allocation_dollars || 0);\r\n      const net = Number(row.net_saturday_am_dollars || 0);\r\n\r\n      grossTotal += gross;\r\n      stripeFeesTotal += fee;\r\n      creatorBountiesTotal += bounty;\r\n      devAllocationTotal += dev;\r\n      netSatAmTotal += net;\r\n\r\n      const cNames: string[] = Array.isArray(row.creator_names)\r\n        ? row.creator_names.filter(Boolean)\r\n        : [];\r\n      if (bounty > 0 && cNames.length > 0) {\r\n        const splitBounty = Number((bounty / cNames.length).toFixed(2));\r\n        cNames.forEach((cName) => {\r\n          const clean = cName.trim();\r\n          const existing = creatorBundleBountyMap.get(clean) || {\r\n            name: clean,\r\n            totalBounty: 0,\r\n            salesCount: 0\r\n          };\r\n          existing.totalBounty += splitBounty;\r\n          existing.salesCount += 1;\r\n          creatorBundleBountyMap.set(clean, existing);\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    return {\r\n      grossTotal,\r\n      stripeFeesTotal,\r\n      creatorBountiesTotal,\r\n      devAllocationTotal,\r\n      netSatAmTotal,\r\n      creatorBundleBounties: Array.from(creatorBundleBountyMap.values()).sort(\r\n        (a, b) => b.totalBounty - a.totalBounty\r\n      )\r\n    };\r\n  }, [storeLedger]);\r\n\r\n  // Calculate Referral Bounties ($5 Creator/Staff, $2 Influencer)\r\n  const referralCommissionSummary = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const partnerByCode = new Map<string, { partnerName: string; role: string; canonicalCode: string }>();\r\n\r\n    creators.forEach(c => {\r\n      const canonicalCode = buildCreatorReferralCode(c.name);\r\n      const variants = getCreatorReferralCodeVariants(c.name);\r\n      variants.forEach(v => {\r\n        partnerByCode.set(v.toUpperCase(), {\r\n          partnerName: c.name,\r\n          role: 'creator',\r\n          canonicalCode\r\n        });\r\n      });\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    partnerProfiles.forEach(p => {\r\n      const displayName = p.linked_creator_name || p.username || 'Partner';\r\n      const rawRole = String(p.role || 'creator').toLowerCase();\r\n      const role = rawRole === 'core_creator' ? 'creator' : rawRole;\r\n      const canonicalCode =\r\n        String(p.referral_code || '').trim().toUpperCase() || buildCreatorReferralCode(displayName);\r\n\r\n      const variants = [\r\n        canonicalCode,\r\n        ...getCreatorReferralCodeVariants(displayName),\r\n        ...getCreatorReferralCodeVariants(p.username)\r\n      ];\r\n      variants.forEach(v => {\r\n        if (v) {\r\n          partnerByCode.set(v.toUpperCase(), {\r\n            partnerName: displayName,\r\n            role,\r\n            canonicalCode\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      });\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    const summaryMap = new Map<string, {\r\n      code: string;\r\n      partnerName: string;\r\n      role: string;\r\n      rate: number;\r\n      referralsCount: number;\r\n      totalCommission: number;\r\n    }>();\r\n\r\n    referredProfiles.forEach((sub) => {\r\n      const rawCode = String(sub.referred_by_code || '').trim().toUpperCase();\r\n      if (!rawCode) return;\r\n      const matchedPartner = partnerByCode.get(rawCode) || partnerByCode.get(`VIP-${rawCode}`);\r\n      const role = matchedPartner?.role || 'creator';\r\n      const rate = role === 'influencer' ? 2 : 5;\r\n      const partnerName = matchedPartner?.partnerName || rawCode;\r\n      const groupKey = matchedPartner?.canonicalCode || rawCode;\r\n\r\n      const existing = summaryMap.get(groupKey) || {\r\n        code: groupKey,\r\n        partnerName,\r\n        role,\r\n        rate,\r\n        referralsCount: 0,\r\n        totalCommission: 0\r\n      };\r\n      existing.referralsCount += 1;\r\n      existing.totalCommission += rate;\r\n      summaryMap.set(groupKey, existing);\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    return Array.from(summaryMap.values()).sort((a, b) => b.totalCommission - a.totalCommission);\r\n  }, [referredProfiles, partnerProfiles, creators]);\r\n\r\n  const availableMonths = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const monthSet = new Set<string>();\r\n    const now = new Date();\r\n    const currentYm = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;\r\n    monthSet.add(currentYm);\r\n\r\n    [...tips, ...storeLedger].forEach((t) => {\r\n      if (t.created_at) {\r\n        const d = new Date(t.created_at);\r\n        if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {\r\n          const ym = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;\r\n          monthSet.add(ym);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    return Array.from(monthSet).sort((a, b) => b.localeCompare(a));\r\n  }, [tips, storeLedger]);\r\n\r\n  const formatMonthLabel = (ym: string) => {\r\n    const [year, month] = ym.split('-');\r\n    const date = new Date(Number(year), Number(month) - 1, 1);\r\n    return date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const lifetimeStats = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const totalLifetimeDollars = tips.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Number(t.amount_dollars || 0), 0);\r\n    const totalTransactions = tips.length;\r\n\r\n    const byCreator = new Map<string, { name: string; total: number; count: number }>();\r\n    tips.forEach((t) => {\r\n      const cName = String(t.creator_name || 'Unknown Creator').trim();\r\n      const existing = byCreator.get(cName) || { name: cName, total: 0, count: 0 };\r\n      existing.total += Number(t.amount_dollars || 0);\r\n      existing.count += 1;\r\n      byCreator.set(cName, existing);\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    const creatorBreakdown = Array.from(byCreator.values()).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);\r\n    const topCreator = creatorBreakdown[0] || null;\r\n\r\n    return {\r\n      totalLifetimeDollars,\r\n      totalTransactions,\r\n      creatorBreakdown,\r\n      topCreator\r\n    };\r\n  }, [tips]);\r\n\r\n  const filteredAndSortedTips = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n\r\n    const filtered = tips.filter((t) => {\r\n      if (selectedMonth !== 'all' && t.created_at) {\r\n        const d = new Date(t.created_at);\r\n        const ym = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;\r\n        if (ym !== selectedMonth) return false;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (selectedCreator !== 'all') {\r\n        if (String(t.creator_name || '').toLowerCase() !== selectedCreator.toLowerCase()) {\r\n          return false;\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (!q) return true;\r\n      return (\r\n        String(t.creator_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        String(t.donor_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        String(t.donor_email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        String(t.fan_message || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        String(t.stripe_session_id || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)\r\n      );\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {\r\n      const timeA = a.created_at ? new Date(a.created_at).getTime() : 0;\r\n      const timeB = b.created_at ? new Date(b.created_at).getTime() : 0;\r\n      const amtA = Number(a.amount_dollars || 0);\r\n      const amtB = Number(b.amount_dollars || 0);\r\n\r\n      switch (sortBy) {\r\n        case 'date_desc':\r\n          return timeB - timeA;\r\n        case 'date_asc':\r\n          return timeA - timeB;\r\n        case 'amount_desc':\r\n          return amtB - amtA;\r\n        case 'amount_asc':\r\n          return amtA - amtB;\r\n        case 'creator_asc':\r\n          return String(a.creator_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.creator_name || ''));\r\n        case 'creator_desc':\r\n          return String(b.creator_name || '').localeCompare(String(a.creator_name || ''));\r\n        case 'donor_asc':\r\n          return String(a.donor_name || a.donor_email || '').localeCompare(String(b.donor_name || b.donor_email || ''));\r\n        case 'donor_desc':\r\n          return String(b.donor_name || b.donor_email || '').localeCompare(String(a.donor_name || a.donor_email || ''));\r\n        default:\r\n          return timeB - timeA;\r\n      }\r\n    });\r\n  }, [tips, selectedMonth, selectedCreator, searchQuery, sortBy]);\r\n\r\n  const filteredStoreLedger = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    return storeLedger.filter((row: any) => {\r\n      if (selectedMonth !== 'all' && row.created_at) {\r\n        const d = new Date(row.created_at);\r\n        const ym = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;\r\n        if (ym !== selectedMonth) return false;\r\n      }\r\n      if (selectedCreator !== 'all') {\r\n        const cNames: string[] = Array.isArray(row.creator_names) ? row.creator_names : [];\r\n        const hasMatch = cNames.some(\r\n          (n) => String(n || '').toLowerCase() === selectedCreator.toLowerCase()\r\n        );\r\n        if (!hasMatch) return false;\r\n      }\r\n      if (!q) return true;\r\n      const cJoined = Array.isArray(row.creator_names) ? row.creator_names.join(' ') : '';\r\n      return (\r\n        String(row.product_title || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        String(row.user_email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\r\n        cJoined.toLowerCase().includes(q)\r\n      );\r\n    });\r\n  }, [storeLedger, selectedMonth, selectedCreator, searchQuery]);\r\n\r\n  const filteredPeriodTotal = useMemo(() => {\r\n    return filteredAndSortedTips.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Number(t.amount_dollars || 0), 0);\r\n  }, [filteredAndSortedTips]);\r\n\r\n  const formatCurrency = (val: number) => {\r\n    return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {\r\n      style: 'currency',\r\n      currency: 'USD',\r\n      minimumFractionDigits: 2\r\n    }).format(val || 0);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleSaveCreatorEmail = async (creatorName: string) => {\r\n    const cleanEmail = String(creatorEmails[creatorName] || '').trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    setSavingCreatorEmail(creatorName);\r\n    try {\r\n      const { error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('series_creators')\r\n        .update({ email: cleanEmail || null })\r\n        .ilike('name', creatorName);\r\n\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      alert(`Saved notification email for ${creatorName}!`);\r\n    } catch (err: any) {\r\n      alert('Error saving creator email: ' + err.message);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setSavingCreatorEmail(null);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleManualAddTip = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\r\n    e.preventDefault();\r\n    if (!newCreatorName.trim() || !newAmount || Number(newAmount) <= 0) {\r\n      return alert('Please select a creator and enter a valid dollar amount.');\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    setIsSubmittingManual(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const matchedCreatorEmail = creatorEmails[newCreatorName.trim()] || null;\r\n      const { error } = await supabase.from('creator_tips').insert([\r\n        {\r\n          creator_name: newCreatorName.trim(),\r\n          creator_email: matchedCreatorEmail,\r\n          donor_name: newDonorName.trim() || 'Saturday AM Supporter',\r\n          donor_email: newDonorEmail.trim().toLowerCase() || null,\r\n          amount_dollars: Number(newAmount),\r\n          fan_message: newFanMessage.trim() || null,\r\n          payout_status: newStatus,\r\n          stripe_session_id: `manual_${Date.now()}`\r\n        }\r\n      ]);\r\n\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      setNewCreatorName('');\r\n      setNewDonorName('');\r\n      setNewDonorEmail('');\r\n      setNewAmount('');\r\n      setNewFanMessage('');\r\n      setShowAddForm(false);\r\n      await fetchPayoutData();\r\n    } catch (err: any) {\r\n      alert('Failed to record transaction: ' + err.message);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setIsSubmittingManual(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleTogglePayoutStatus = async (tip: any) => {\r\n    const nextStatus = tip.payout_status === 'paid' ? 'pending' : 'paid';\r\n    const { error } = await supabase\r\n      .from('creator_tips')\r\n      .update({ payout_status: nextStatus })\r\n      .eq('id', tip.id);\r\n\r\n    if (!error) {\r\n      setTips((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === tip.id ? { ...t, payout_status: nextStatus } : t)));\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleToggleStoreLedgerStatus = async (row: any) => {\r\n    const nextStatus = row.status === 'paid' || row.status === 'completed' ? 'pending' : 'completed';\r\n    const { error } = await supabase\r\n      .from('store_payout_ledger')\r\n      .update({ status: nextStatus })\r\n      .eq('id', row.id);\r\n    if (!error) {\r\n      setStoreLedger((prev) =>\r\n        prev.map((r) => (r.id === row.id ? { ...r, status: nextStatus } : r))\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleDeleteTip = async (id: string) => {\r\n    if (!window.confirm('Remove this transaction from the payout ledger?')) return;\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.from('creator_tips').delete().eq('id', id);\r\n    if (!error) {\r\n      setTips((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleDeleteStoreLedgerRow = async (id: string) => {\r\n    if (!window.confirm('Remove this store sale entry from the ledger?')) return;\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.from('store_payout_ledger').delete().eq('id', id);\r\n    if (!error) {\r\n      setStoreLedger((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== id));\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleExportCSV = () => {\r\n    const escapeCsv = (val: any) => `\"${String(val ?? '').replace(/\"/g, '\"\"')}\"`;\r\n\r\n    if (ledgerTab === 'store') {\r\n      const headers = [\r\n        'Date',\r\n        'Product Title',\r\n        'Product Type',\r\n        'Buyer Email',\r\n        'Gross (USD)',\r\n        'Stripe Fee (USD)',\r\n        'Creator Bounty ($5 Flat)',\r\n        'Attributed Creators',\r\n        'Dev Allocation (USD)',\r\n        'Saturday AM Net Retained (USD)',\r\n        'Status'\r\n      ];\r\n      const rows = filteredStoreLedger.map((r) =>\r\n        [\r\n          escapeCsv(r.created_at ? new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleString() : ''),\r\n          escapeCsv(r.product_title),\r\n          escapeCsv(r.product_type),\r\n          escapeCsv(r.user_email || ''),\r\n          escapeCsv(Number(r.gross_amount_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n          escapeCsv(Number(r.stripe_fee_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n          escapeCsv(Number(r.creator_bounty_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n          escapeCsv(Array.isArray(r.creator_names) ? r.creator_names.join(' & ') : ''),\r\n          escapeCsv(Number(r.dev_allocation_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n          escapeCsv(Number(r.net_saturday_am_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n          escapeCsv(r.status || 'completed')\r\n        ].join(',')\r\n      );\r\n      const csvContent = 'data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,' + [headers.join(','), ...rows].join('\\n');\r\n      const link = document.createElement('a');\r\n      link.setAttribute('href', encodeURI(csvContent));\r\n      link.setAttribute(\r\n        'download',\r\n        `SaturdayAM_Store_Bundle_Ledger_${selectedMonth === 'all' ? 'AllTime' : selectedMonth}.csv`\r\n      );\r\n      document.body.appendChild(link);\r\n      link.click();\r\n      document.body.removeChild(link);\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const headers = [\r\n      'Date',\r\n      'Creator Name',\r\n      'Creator Email',\r\n      'Amount (USD)',\r\n      'Payout Status',\r\n      'Donor Name',\r\n      'Donor Email',\r\n      'Fan Mail Message',\r\n      'Transaction ID'\r\n    ];\r\n\r\n    const rows = filteredAndSortedTips.map((t) =>\r\n      [\r\n        escapeCsv(t.created_at ? new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleString() : ''),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.creator_name),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.creator_email || creatorEmails[t.creator_name] || ''),\r\n        escapeCsv(Number(t.amount_dollars || 0).toFixed(2)),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.payout_status || 'paid'),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.donor_name || 'Anonymous Fan'),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.donor_email || ''),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.fan_message || ''),\r\n        escapeCsv(t.stripe_session_id || t.id)\r\n      ].join(',')\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    const csvContent = 'data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,' + [headers.join(','), ...rows].join('\\n');\r\n    const encodedUri = encodeURI(csvContent);\r\n    const link = document.createElement('a');\r\n    link.setAttribute('href', encodedUri);\r\n    link.setAttribute(\r\n      'download',\r\n      `SaturdayAM_Creator_Payouts_${selectedMonth === 'all' ? 'AllTime' : selectedMonth}.csv`\r\n    );\r\n    document.body.appendChild(link);\r\n    link.click();\r\n    document.body.removeChild(link);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-lg max-w-5xl animate-fade-in space-y-6\">\r\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-zinc-800 pb-4\">\r\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\r\n          <DollarSign className=\"w-6 h-6 text-[#fe9a00]\" />\r\n          <div>\r\n            <h2 className=\"text-xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-widest text-[#fe9a00]\">\r\n              Creator Payouts, Bundle Bounties & Dev Split Ledger\r\n            </h2>\r\n            <p className=\"text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest\">\r\n              Track 100% creator tips, $5.00 Annual Bundle/Sub Bounties, 100% Platform Skins/Packs & Dev Allocations\r\n            </p>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowEmailDirectory((prev) => !prev)}\r\n            className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${\r\n              showEmailDirectory\r\n                ? 'bg-[#fe9a00] text-black border-[#fe9a00]'\r\n                : 'bg-black text-zinc-300 border-zinc-700 hover:border-[#fe9a00] hover:text-white'\r\n            }`}\r\n          >\r\n            <Mail className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" /> Creator Alert Emails\r\n          </button>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowAddForm((prev) => !prev)}\r\n            className=\"px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-black border border-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-[#fe9a00] hover:border-[#fe9a00] text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n          >\r\n            <PlusCircle className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" /> Log Payout / Tip\r\n          </button>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={handleExportCSV}\r\n            className=\"px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-black border border-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-[#fe9a00] hover:border-[#fe9a00] text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n            title=\"Export active ledger view to CSV\"\r\n          >\r\n            <Download className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" /> Export CSV\r\n          </button>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={fetchPayoutData}\r\n            className=\"p-2 bg-black border border-zinc-700 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:border-[#fe9a00] transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n            title=\"Refresh Ledger\"\r\n          >\r\n            <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin text-[#fe9a00]' : ''}`} />\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {/* =====================================================================\r\n          IN-APP STORE & BUNDLE ECONOMICS SUMMARY (SECTION 5 & 6 RULES)\r\n      ===================================================================== */}\r\n      <div className=\"bg-black border border-[#fe9a00]/50 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4\">\r\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-zinc-800 pb-3\">\r\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\r\n            <ShoppingBag className=\"w-5 h-5 text-[#fe9a00]\" />\r\n            <div>\r\n              <h3 className=\"text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white\">\r\n                In-App Store & Annual Bundle Economics ({storeLedger.length} Sales)\r\n              </h3>\r\n              <p className=\"text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-bold\">\r\n                Bundles: Flat $5.00 Creator Bounty + $1.50 Dev Allocation • Individual Skins ($10+) & Avatar Packs ($5+): 100% Platform Revenue\r\n              </p>\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={() => setLedgerTab(ledgerTab === 'store' ? 'tips' : 'store')}\r\n            className=\"px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#fe9a00]/15 hover:bg-[#fe9a00] text-[#fe9a00] hover:text-black border border-[#fe9a00]/40 text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors cursor-pointer self-start sm:self-auto\"\r\n          >\r\n            {ledgerTab === 'store' ? }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchAnalytics();\r\n\r\n    const subscription = supabase\r\n      .channel('realtime_analytics_v5')\r\n      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', startOfQuarter.setDate(startOfToday.getDate() - 90);\r\n    const startOfLastQuarter = new Date(startOfQuarter);\r\n    startOfLastQuarter.setDate(startOfQuarter.getDate() - 90);\r\n\r\n    const startOfLastYear.setDate(startOfYear.getDate() - 365);\r\n\r\n    const profileDates = activeProfiles\r\n      .map(p => {\r\n        const raw = p.created_at || p.updated_at;\r\n        return raw ? plusWeekAnnual: countDatesBetween(annualPlusDates, startOfWeek),\r\n\r\n      plusMonth: countDatesBetween(plusSignupDates, startOfMonth),\r\n      plusLastMonth: countDatesBetween(plusSignupDates,    .slice(0, 8);\r\n\r\n    const historyRows = (rawTables.readingHistory || []).filter(matchesAudience);\r\n    const completedRows = (rawTables.completedReads || []).filter(matchesAudience);\r\n   const telemetryRows = (rawTables.telemetryEvents || []).filter(matchesAudience);\r\n\r\n    // --- UNIFIED DE-DUPLICATED HYPES ---\r\n    const hypesFingerprints = new Set<string>();\r\n    const visitorsLastMonth: vLastMonth,\r\n      convMonthPct: calcConvPct(growthStats.newUsersMonth, vMonth),\r\n\r\n      visitorsLifetime: vLifetime,\r\n      convLifetimePct: calcConvPct(totalUsers, };\r\n\r\n    historyRows.forEach((r: any) => registerActiveEvent(r.user_id, r.updated_at || r.created_at));\r\n    completedRows.forEach((r: any) => registerActiveEvent(r.user_id, r.created_at));\r\n });\r\n      const bucket: DailyChartBucket = {\r\n        dateKey: key,\r\n        label,\r\n        shortLabel,\r\n        reads: 0,\r\n        completed: 0,\r\n        signups: 0,\r\n (creatorName?: string, dbStripeId?: string): string => {\r\n  const cleanDb = String(dbStripeId || '').trim();\r\n\r\n  // 1. If the Command Center saved a valid Stripe Connect ID in Supabase, it ALWAYS wins over hardcoded defaults!\r\n  if (cleanDb.startsWith('acct_')) {\r\n    return cleanDb;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // 3. (!visibilityMap.has(key)) {\r\n            visibilityMap.set(key, rowVisible);\r\n          } else if (rowVisible) {\r\n            visibilityMap.set(key, true);\r\n          }\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        const creatorMap = new Map<string, CreatorProfile>();\r\n\r\n        (creatorRows || []).forEach((row: any) => {\r\n          if (!row.name) return;\r\n          const key = normalizeCreatorKey(row.name);\r\n          if (visibilityMap.get(key) === false) return;\r\n\r\n          const displayName = key === 'erick pedrucci' ?
